---
title: "Learning Lab — Practice Tools for Clinical Research"
sidebarTitle: "Learning Lab"
mode: "wide"
keywords: ["practice", "scenarios", "knowledge check", "team exercise", "inspection prep", "judgment calls"]
---

import { useEffect } from 'react'

export const LL_CSS = `:root {
  --navy:        #2b2666;
  --navy-card:   #2b2666;
  --teal:        var(--ri-accent, #2b9ce2);
  --teal-bright: var(--ri-accent, #74c0f8);
  --teal-chip:   #a8d8f5;
  --teal-ll:     #e8f4fc;
  --sky:         #2b9ce2;
  --sky-l:       #e5f3fb;
  --amber:       #ffb400;
  --amber-l:     #fff3cc;
  --coral:       #e63946;
  --coral-l:     #fde8ea;
  --purple:      #9039f9;
  --purple-l:    #ede3fe;
  --fuchsia:     #f050d0;
  --fuchsia-l:   #fce8f9;
  --green:       #1d9e75;
  --green-l:     #e5f5ee;
  --bg:          #f6f6f7;
  --bg-low:      #f0f0f2;
  --bg-cont:     #eaeaec;
  --bg-high:     #e4e4e7;
  --bg-highest:  #dededf;
  --white:       #ffffff;
  --text:        #1b1c1a;
  --textm:       #474650;
  --textd:       #787681;
  --bdr:         #c6c5ce;
  --bdr-soft:    #e0dfe6;
  --shadow:      0 12px 32px -4px rgba(27,28,26,.06);
}

*, *::before, *::after { box-sizing: border-box; }
:root { --green-bright: #27ae60; --green-bright-l: #eafaf1; --rose: #ff537f; --rose-l: #ffe8ee; }

.kp-hero { max-width: 1200px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 52px 32px 40px; position: relative; }
.kp-hero::after { content: ''; display: block; width: 720px; max-width: 100%; height: 1px; background: var(--bdr-soft); margin-top: 40px; }
.kp-eyebrow { font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .2em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--teal); margin-bottom: 12px; }
.kp-h1 { font-size: clamp(28px,4vw,46px); font-weight: 800; color: var(--navy); letter-spacing: -.03em; line-height: 1.05; margin-bottom: 14px; }
.kp-h1 em { font-style: normal; color: var(--sky); }
.kp-sub { font-size: 15px; color: var(--textm); line-height: 1.75; max-width: 540px; margin-bottom: 18px; }
.kp-informal { font-size: 11px; color: var(--textd); background: var(--bg-low); border: 1px solid var(--bdr-soft); padding: 6px 12px; display: inline-block; }
.kp-stage { max-width: 1200px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 16px 32px 100px; min-height: 560px; overflow-x: hidden; }
.kp-phase { display: none; }
.kp-phase.phase-active { display: block; }
@keyframes phaseIn   { from { opacity:0; transform:translateY(32px); } to { opacity:1; transform:translateY(0); } }
@keyframes phaseOut  { from { opacity:1; transform:translateY(0); }    to { opacity:0; transform:translateY(-18px); } }
@keyframes phaseBack { from { opacity:0; transform:translateY(-24px); } to { opacity:1; transform:translateY(0); } }
@keyframes cardRise  { to   { opacity:1; transform:translateY(0); } }
@keyframes ratIn     { from { opacity:0; transform:translateY(6px); } to { opacity:1; transform:none; } }
.kp-phase-label { font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .22em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--textd); margin-bottom: 24px; }
.kp-back { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 7px; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .06em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--textd); background: none; border: none; cursor: pointer; padding: 0; margin-bottom: 32px; transition: color .15s, transform .2s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1); }
.kp-back:hover { color: var(--navy); transform: translateX(-3px); }
.kp-nav-row { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 20px; margin-bottom: 32px; }
.kp-nav-row .kp-back { margin-bottom: 0; }
.kp-nav-row-sep { width: 1px; height: 14px; background: var(--bdr-soft); }
.kp-home-link { font-size: 11px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .06em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--textd); background: none; border: none; cursor: pointer; padding: 0; display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; transition: color .15s; }
.kp-home-link:hover { color: var(--navy); }
.kp-home-link svg { opacity: .5; }
.kp-entry-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 20px; max-width: 820px; }
.kp-entry-card { background: var(--white); border-radius: 12px; border: 1.5px solid var(--bdr-soft); padding: 40px 36px 34px; cursor: pointer; position: relative; overflow: hidden; box-shadow: 0 2px 14px rgba(21,14,81,.05); opacity: 0; transform: translateY(24px); animation: cardRise .48s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1) forwards; transition: transform .3s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1), box-shadow .3s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1), border-color .2s; }
.kp-entry-card:nth-child(1) { animation-delay: .07s; }
.kp-entry-card:nth-child(2) { animation-delay: .15s; }
.kp-entry-card:hover { transform: translateY(-6px); box-shadow: 0 20px 48px rgba(21,14,81,.12); border-color: var(--bdr); }
.kp-entry-card::before { content: ''; position: absolute; inset: 0; border-radius: 12px; opacity: 0; transition: opacity .3s; pointer-events: none; }
.kp-entry-card.browse::before  { background: radial-gradient(ellipse at 110% 110%, rgba(0,196,180,.09) 0%, transparent 65%); }
.kp-entry-card.shuffle::before { background: radial-gradient(ellipse at 110% 110%, rgba(144,57,249,.08) 0%, transparent 65%); }
.kp-entry-card:hover::before { opacity: 1; }
.kp-entry-accent { height: 3px; border-radius: 2px; margin-bottom: 28px; transition: width .3s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1); }
.kp-entry-card.browse  .kp-entry-accent { width: 28px; background: var(--teal-bright); }
.kp-entry-card.shuffle .kp-entry-accent { width: 28px; background: var(--purple); }
.kp-entry-card:hover   .kp-entry-accent { width: 52px; }
.kp-entry-mode { font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .16em; text-transform: uppercase; margin-bottom: 10px; }
.kp-entry-card.browse  .kp-entry-mode { color: var(--teal); }
.kp-entry-card.shuffle .kp-entry-mode { color: var(--purple); }
.kp-entry-title { font-size: 22px; font-weight: 800; color: var(--navy); letter-spacing: -.02em; line-height: 1.15; margin-bottom: 12px; }
.kp-entry-desc  { font-size: 13.5px; color: var(--textm); line-height: 1.7; margin-bottom: 28px; }
.kp-entry-meta { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; padding-top: 20px; border-top: 1px solid var(--bdr-soft); }
.kp-entry-stat { font-size: 11px; font-weight: 700; color: var(--textd); }
.kp-entry-stat strong { font-size: 15px; display: block; margin-bottom: 1px; }
.kp-entry-card.browse  .kp-entry-stat strong { color: var(--teal); }
.kp-entry-card.shuffle .kp-entry-stat strong { color: var(--purple); }
.kp-entry-sep  { width: 1px; height: 28px; background: var(--bdr-soft); flex-shrink: 0; }
.kp-entry-cta  { margin-left: auto; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .05em; text-transform: uppercase; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 5px; }
.kp-entry-card.browse  .kp-entry-cta { color: var(--teal); }
.kp-entry-card.shuffle .kp-entry-cta { color: var(--purple); }
.kp-entry-cta-arr { display: inline-block; transition: transform .25s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1); }
.kp-entry-card:hover .kp-entry-cta-arr { transform: translateX(4px); }
.kp-studytype-wrap { max-width: 680px; }
.kp-studytype-intro { margin-bottom: 32px; }
.kp-studytype-label { font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .2em; text-transform: uppercase; margin-bottom: 10px; }
.kp-studytype-label.browse-col  { color: var(--teal); }
.kp-studytype-label.shuffle-col { color: var(--purple); }
.kp-studytype-title { font-size: clamp(20px,3vw,30px); font-weight: 800; color: var(--navy); letter-spacing: -.02em; line-height: 1.1; margin-bottom: 8px; }
.kp-studytype-sub { font-size: 13.5px; color: var(--textm); line-height: 1.65; }
.kp-studytype-chips { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 12px; }
.kp-studytype-chip { background: var(--white); border-radius: 10px; border: 1.5px solid var(--bdr-soft); padding: 22px 26px; cursor: pointer; display: flex; align-items: flex-start; gap: 18px; box-shadow: 0 1px 6px rgba(21,14,81,.04); opacity: 0; transform: translateY(16px); animation: cardRise .42s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1) forwards; transition: transform .25s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1), box-shadow .25s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1), border-color .18s; }
.kp-studytype-chip:nth-child(1) { animation-delay: .06s; }
.kp-studytype-chip:nth-child(2) { animation-delay: .13s; }
.kp-studytype-chip:hover { transform: translateY(-3px); box-shadow: 0 8px 24px rgba(21,14,81,.1); border-color: var(--bdr); }
.kp-studytype-chip-dot { width: 10px; height: 10px; border-radius: 50%; flex-shrink: 0; margin-top: 4px; }
.kp-studytype-chip[data-type="interventional"] .kp-studytype-chip-dot { background: var(--sky); }
.kp-studytype-chip[data-type="observational"]  .kp-studytype-chip-dot { background: var(--green); }
.kp-studytype-chip-name { font-size: 15px; font-weight: 700; color: var(--navy); margin-bottom: 4px; }
.kp-studytype-chip-desc { font-size: 12.5px; color: var(--textm); line-height: 1.6; }
.kp-studytype-chip-tags { display: flex; gap: 5px; flex-wrap: wrap; margin-top: 10px; }
.kp-studytype-tag { font-size: 10px; font-weight: 700; padding: 2px 8px; border-radius: 9999px; }
.kp-studytype-chip[data-type="interventional"] .kp-studytype-tag { background: var(--sky-l); color: var(--sky); }
.kp-studytype-chip[data-type="observational"]  .kp-studytype-tag { background: var(--green-l); color: var(--green); }
.kp-topic-intro { margin-bottom: 28px; }
.kp-topic-intro-label { font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .2em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--teal); margin-bottom: 8px; }
.kp-topic-intro-title { font-size: clamp(20px,3vw,30px); font-weight: 800; color: var(--navy); letter-spacing: -.02em; line-height: 1.1; }
.kp-topic-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(3,1fr); gap: 12px; }
.kp-topic-tile { background: var(--white); border-radius: 8px; border: 1.5px solid var(--bdr-soft); padding: 20px 20px 18px; cursor: pointer; box-shadow: 0 1px 6px rgba(21,14,81,.04); opacity: 0; transform: translateY(20px); animation: cardRise .44s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1) forwards; transition: transform .25s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1), box-shadow .25s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1), border-color .18s; }
.kp-topic-tile:nth-child(1) { animation-delay: .06s; }
.kp-topic-tile:nth-child(2) { animation-delay: .11s; }
.kp-topic-tile:nth-child(3) { animation-delay: .16s; }
.kp-topic-tile:nth-child(4) { animation-delay: .21s; }
.kp-topic-tile:nth-child(5) { animation-delay: .26s; }
.kp-topic-tile:nth-child(6) { animation-delay: .31s; }
.kp-topic-tile:hover { transform: translateY(-4px); box-shadow: 0 10px 28px rgba(21,14,81,.11); border-color: var(--teal-bright); }
.kp-topic-tile.locked { opacity: .42; cursor: default; pointer-events: none; }
.kp-tt-name  { font-size: 14px; font-weight: 700; color: var(--navy); line-height: 1.3; margin-bottom: 5px; }
.kp-tt-sop   { font-size: 10px; font-family: "Courier New", monospace; color: var(--textd); margin-bottom: 12px; }
.kp-tt-pills { display: flex; gap: 5px; flex-wrap: wrap; }
.kp-tt-pill  { font-size: 10px; font-weight: 700; padding: 2px 9px; border-radius: 9999px; }
.kp-tt-pill.checks     { background: var(--teal-bright); color: var(--navy); }
.kp-tt-pill.scenarios  { background: var(--purple); color: white; }
.kp-tt-pill.inspection { background: var(--sky); color: white; }
.kp-tt-pill.pending    { background: var(--bg-high); color: var(--textd); }
.kp-seq-header { display: flex; align-items: flex-start; justify-content: space-between; gap: 20px; margin-bottom: 28px; }
.kp-seq-title  { font-size: clamp(16px,2.5vw,22px); font-weight: 800; color: var(--navy); letter-spacing: -.02em; line-height: 1.15; }
.kp-seq-meta   { font-size: 11px; font-family: "Courier New", monospace; color: var(--textd); margin-top: 5px; }
.kp-seq-progress-row { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 14px; margin-bottom: 28px; }
.kp-seq-progress-bar { flex: 1; height: 3px; background: var(--bg-high); border-radius: 2px; overflow: hidden; }
.kp-seq-progress-fill { height: 100%; border-radius: 2px; transition: width .4s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1); }
.kp-seq-progress-fill.browse-col  { background: var(--teal-bright); }
.kp-seq-progress-fill.shuffle-col { background: var(--purple); }
.kp-seq-progress-label { font-size: 11px; font-weight: 700; color: var(--textd); white-space: nowrap; flex-shrink: 0; }
.kp-qcard { background: var(--white); border-radius: 12px; border: 1.5px solid var(--bdr-soft); overflow: hidden; box-shadow: 0 4px 20px rgba(21,14,81,.07); animation: qcardIn .38s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1); }
@keyframes qcardIn  { from { opacity:0; transform:translateY(18px) scale(.99); } to { opacity:1; transform:none; } }
@keyframes qcardOut { from { opacity:1; transform:none; } to { opacity:0; transform:translateY(-14px) scale(.99); } }
.kp-qcard.exiting { animation: qcardOut .22s cubic-bezier(.4,0,1,1) forwards; }
.kp-qcard-type { padding: 16px 24px 0; font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .16em; text-transform: uppercase; }
.kp-qcard-type.check    { color: var(--teal); }
.kp-qcard-type.scenario   { color: var(--purple); }
.kp-qcard-type.inspection { color: var(--sky); }
.kp-qcard-type.grouped    { color: var(--purple); letter-spacing: .1em; }
.kp-insp-cols { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; min-height: 260px; }
.kp-insp-left { padding: 28px 28px 28px 28px; border-right: 1px solid var(--bdr-soft); display: flex; flex-direction: column; justify-content: center; }
.kp-insp-right { padding: 28px 28px 28px 28px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 16px; }
.kp-insp-right .kp-qcard-rat { margin: 0; border-radius: 6px; }
@media (max-width: 700px) { .kp-insp-cols { grid-template-columns: 1fr; } .kp-insp-left { border-right: none; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--bdr-soft); justify-content: flex-start; } }
.kp-insp-prompt { font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .16em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--textd); margin-bottom: 16px; }
.kp-insp-q { font-size: 16px; font-weight: 700; color: var(--navy); line-height: 1.6; }
.kp-insp-prep { font-size: 13px; color: var(--textm); line-height: 1.65; background: var(--bg-low); border-radius: 6px; padding: 12px 14px; }
.kp-insp-prep::before { content: 'Think about it: '; font-weight: 700; color: var(--navy); }
.inspection-btn { background: var(--sky) !important; width: 100%; }
.inspection-btn:hover { opacity: .88; }
.inspection-rat { background: var(--white); border-color: var(--bdr-soft); }
.inspection-rat .kp-qcard-rat-label { color: var(--green-bright); }
.kp-insp-gap { margin-top: 18px; padding-top: 16px; border-top: 1px solid var(--bdr-soft); }
.kp-insp-gap-label { font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .16em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--coral); margin-bottom: 6px; }
.kp-insp-gap-text { font-size: 13px; color: var(--textm); line-height: 1.65; }
.kp-seq-progress-fill.inspection-col { background: var(--sky); }
.kp-qcard-topic { padding: 3px 24px 0; font-size: 10.5px; color: var(--textd); font-family: "Courier New", monospace; }
.kp-qcard-q    { padding: 12px 24px 18px; font-size: 15px; font-weight: 600; color: var(--navy); line-height: 1.55; }
.kp-qcard-opts { padding: 0 24px 24px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px; }
.kp-qopt { padding: 11px 16px; background: var(--bg-low); border-radius: 6px; border: 1.5px solid transparent; cursor: pointer; display: flex; align-items: flex-start; gap: 12px; font-size: 13px; color: var(--textm); line-height: 1.45; -webkit-user-select: none; user-select: none; transition: background .18s, border-color .18s, box-shadow .2s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1), transform .18s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1); }
.kp-qopt:hover { background: var(--white); border-color: var(--navy); box-shadow: 0 3px 12px rgba(21,14,81,.08); transform: translateX(3px); }
.kp-qopt-key { font-size: 10px; font-weight: 700; background: var(--white); border: 1.5px solid var(--bdr-soft); border-radius: 4px; padding: 1px 7px; min-width: 26px; text-align: center; color: var(--textd); flex-shrink: 0; margin-top: 1px; transition: background .18s, color .18s, border-color .18s; }
.kp-qopt:hover .kp-qopt-key { background: var(--navy); color: white; border-color: var(--navy); }
.kp-qopt.correct { background: var(--green-bright-l); border-color: var(--green-bright); transform: none; cursor: default; }
.kp-qopt.correct .kp-qopt-key { background: var(--green-bright); color: white; border-color: var(--green-bright); }
.kp-qopt.wrong   { background: var(--coral-l); border-color: var(--coral); transform: none; cursor: default; opacity: .65; }
.kp-qopt.wrong   .kp-qopt-key { background: var(--coral); color: white; border-color: var(--coral); }
.kp-qopt.neutral-out { opacity: .32; cursor: default; transform: none; }
.kp-qopt.disabled    { pointer-events: none; }
.kp-qcard-reveal-wrap { padding: 0 24px 24px; }
.kp-qcard-reveal-btn { width: 100%; padding: 13px 20px; background: var(--purple); color: white; border: none; border-radius: 6px; cursor: pointer; font-size: 12px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .06em; text-transform: uppercase; box-shadow: 0 2px 10px rgba(144,57,249,.25); transition: background .18s, transform .2s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1), box-shadow .2s; }
.kp-qcard-reveal-btn:hover { background: var(--navy); transform: scale(1.02); box-shadow: 0 4px 16px rgba(21,14,81,.18); }
.kp-qcard-rat { display: none; margin: 0 24px 0; padding: 16px 20px 18px; border-radius: 6px; border: 1px solid var(--bdr-soft); }
.kp-qcard-rat.check-rat    { background: linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(0,106,97,.04) 0%,transparent 60%); }
.kp-qcard-rat.scenario-rat { background: linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(144,57,249,.04) 0%,transparent 60%); }
.kp-qcard-rat.visible { display: block; animation: ratIn .28s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1); }
.kp-qcard-rat-label { font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .14em; text-transform: uppercase; margin-bottom: 7px; }
.kp-qcard-rat.check-rat    .kp-qcard-rat-label { color: var(--teal); }
.kp-qcard-rat.scenario-rat .kp-qcard-rat-label { color: var(--purple); }
.kp-qcard-rat-text { font-size: 13px; color: var(--textm); line-height: 1.7; margin-bottom: 8px; }
.kp-qcard-rat-ref  { font-size: 11px; color: var(--textd); }
.kp-qcard-rat-ref a { color: var(--teal); font-weight: 600; text-decoration: none; }
.kp-qcard-rat-ref a:hover { text-decoration: underline; }
.kp-qcard-next-wrap { padding: 20px 24px 28px; display: none; }
.kp-qcard-next-wrap.visible { display: block; }
.kp-qcard-next { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; padding: 12px 24px; background: var(--navy); color: white; border: none; border-radius: 6px; cursor: pointer; font-size: 12px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .06em; text-transform: uppercase; box-shadow: 0 2px 12px rgba(21,14,81,.18); transition: background .18s, transform .2s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1), box-shadow .2s; }
.kp-qcard-next:hover { background: var(--navy-card); transform: translateY(-2px); box-shadow: 0 6px 20px rgba(21,14,81,.22); }
.kp-end { max-width: 560px; }
.kp-end-eyebrow { font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .2em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--textd); margin-bottom: 20px; }
.kp-end-score { margin-bottom: 28px; }
.kp-end-score-num { font-size: clamp(48px,8vw,72px); font-weight: 800; color: var(--navy); letter-spacing: -.04em; line-height: 1; margin-bottom: 4px; }
.kp-end-score-num span { color: var(--teal); font-size: .65em; }
.kp-end-score-label { font-size: 15px; font-weight: 700; color: var(--textm); }
.kp-end-feedback { font-size: 14px; color: var(--textm); line-height: 1.65; margin-bottom: 28px; max-width: 480px; }
.kp-end-breakdown { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 10px; margin-bottom: 36px; }
.kp-end-row { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; padding: 14px 18px; background: var(--white); border-radius: 8px; border: 1.5px solid var(--bdr-soft); }
.kp-end-row-label { font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600; color: var(--textm); }
.kp-end-row-val { font-size: 14px; font-weight: 800; color: var(--navy); }
.kp-end-row-val.good { color: var(--green); }
.kp-end-row-val.ok   { color: var(--amber); }
.kp-end-row-val.low  { color: var(--coral); }
.kp-end-actions { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px; flex-wrap: wrap; }
.kp-end-btn-primary { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 7px; padding: 12px 24px; background: var(--navy); color: white; border: none; border-radius: 6px; cursor: pointer; font-size: 12px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .06em; text-transform: uppercase; box-shadow: 0 2px 12px rgba(21,14,81,.18); transition: background .18s, transform .2s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1); }
.kp-end-btn-primary:hover { background: var(--navy-card); transform: translateY(-2px); }
.kp-end-btn-ghost { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 7px; padding: 11px 22px; background: none; color: var(--textm); border: 1.5px solid var(--bdr-soft); border-radius: 6px; cursor: pointer; font-size: 12px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .06em; text-transform: uppercase; transition: border-color .18s, color .18s; }
.kp-end-btn-ghost:hover { border-color: var(--navy); color: var(--navy); }
.kp-mode-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 16px; max-width: 720px; }
.kp-mode-card { background: var(--white); border-radius: 10px; border: 1.5px solid var(--bdr-soft); padding: 32px 28px 26px; cursor: pointer; position: relative; overflow: hidden; box-shadow: 0 2px 12px rgba(21,14,81,.05); opacity: 0; transform: translateY(20px); animation: cardRise .44s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1) forwards; transition: transform .28s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1), box-shadow .28s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1), border-color .2s; }
.kp-mode-card:nth-child(1) { animation-delay: .07s; }
.kp-mode-card:nth-child(2) { animation-delay: .14s; }
.kp-mode-card:hover { transform: translateY(-5px); box-shadow: 0 14px 36px rgba(21,14,81,.11); border-color: var(--bdr); }
.kp-mode-card::before { content: ''; position: absolute; inset: 0; border-radius: 10px; opacity: 0; transition: opacity .3s; pointer-events: none; }
.kp-mode-card.checks::before  { background: radial-gradient(ellipse at 110% 110%, rgba(0,196,180,.09) 0%, transparent 65%); }
.kp-mode-card.scenarios::before { background: radial-gradient(ellipse at 110% 110%, rgba(144,57,249,.08) 0%, transparent 65%); }
.kp-mode-card:hover::before { opacity: 1; }
.kp-mode-card-accent { height: 3px; border-radius: 2px; margin-bottom: 22px; transition: width .28s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1); }
.kp-mode-card.checks   .kp-mode-card-accent { width: 28px; background: var(--teal-bright); }
.kp-mode-card.scenarios .kp-mode-card-accent { width: 28px; background: var(--purple); }
.kp-mode-card:hover .kp-mode-card-accent { width: 48px; }
.kp-mode-card-type { font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .16em; text-transform: uppercase; margin-bottom: 8px; }
.kp-mode-card.checks    .kp-mode-card-type { color: var(--teal); }
.kp-mode-card.scenarios .kp-mode-card-type { color: var(--purple); }
.kp-mode-card.inspection .kp-mode-card-type { color: var(--sky); }
.kp-mode-card.checks    .kp-mode-card-arr { color: var(--teal); }
.kp-mode-card.scenarios .kp-mode-card-arr { color: var(--purple); }
.kp-mode-card.inspection .kp-mode-card-arr { color: var(--sky); }
.kp-mode-card-title { font-size: 19px; font-weight: 800; color: var(--navy); letter-spacing: -.02em; line-height: 1.15; margin-bottom: 10px; }
.kp-mode-card-desc { font-size: 13px; color: var(--textm); line-height: 1.7; margin-bottom: 24px; }
.kp-mode-card-meta { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; padding-top: 16px; border-top: 1px solid var(--bdr-soft); }
.kp-mode-card-count { font-size: 12px; font-weight: 700; color: var(--textd); }
.kp-mode-card-arr { font-size: 14px; font-weight: 700; transition: transform .22s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1); }
.kp-mode-card:hover .kp-mode-card-arr { transform: translateX(5px); }
.kp-landing-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr); gap: 16px; max-width: 720px; }
.kp-landing-card { background: var(--white); border-radius: 12px; border: 1.5px solid var(--bdr-soft); padding: 32px 28px 26px; cursor: pointer; position: relative; overflow: hidden; box-shadow: 0 2px 14px rgba(21,14,81,.05); opacity: 0; transform: translateY(24px); animation: cardRise .48s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1) forwards; transition: transform .3s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1), box-shadow .3s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1), border-color .2s; }
.kp-landing-card:nth-child(1) { animation-delay: .07s; }
.kp-landing-card:nth-child(2) { animation-delay: .14s; }
.kp-landing-card:nth-child(3) { animation-delay: .21s; }
.kp-landing-card:nth-child(4) { animation-delay: .28s; }
.kp-landing-card:nth-child(5) { animation-delay: .35s; }
.kp-landing-card:hover { transform: translateY(-6px); box-shadow: 0 20px 48px rgba(21,14,81,.12); border-color: var(--bdr); }
.kp-landing-accent { height: 3px; border-radius: 2px; width: 28px; margin-bottom: 20px; transition: width .28s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1); }
.kp-landing-card:hover .kp-landing-accent { width: 52px; }
.kp-landing-card.practice .kp-landing-accent { background: var(--teal-bright); }
.kp-landing-card.team     .kp-landing-accent { background: var(--fuchsia); }
.kp-landing-card.docs     .kp-landing-accent { background: var(--purple); }
.kp-landing-card.jc       .kp-landing-accent { background: var(--rose); }
.kp-landing-card.insp     .kp-landing-accent { background: var(--sky); }
.kp-landing-mode { font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .16em; text-transform: uppercase; margin-bottom: 8px; }
.kp-landing-card.practice .kp-landing-mode { color: var(--teal); }
.kp-landing-card.team     .kp-landing-mode { color: var(--fuchsia); }
.kp-landing-card.docs     .kp-landing-mode { color: var(--purple); }
.kp-landing-card.jc       .kp-landing-mode { color: var(--rose); }
.kp-landing-card.insp     .kp-landing-mode { color: var(--sky); }
.kp-landing-title { font-size: 19px; font-weight: 800; color: var(--navy); letter-spacing: -.02em; line-height: 1.15; margin-bottom: 10px; }
.kp-landing-desc { font-size: 13px; color: var(--textm); line-height: 1.7; margin-bottom: 24px; flex: 1; }
.kp-landing-meta { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; padding-top: 16px; border-top: 1px solid var(--bdr-soft); }
.kp-landing-stat { font-size: 11px; font-weight: 700; color: var(--textd); }
.kp-landing-stat strong { font-size: 15px; display: block; margin-bottom: 1px; color: var(--teal); }
.kp-landing-sep { width: 1px; height: 20px; background: var(--bdr-soft); }
.kp-landing-cta { margin-left: auto; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .05em; text-transform: uppercase; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 5px; }
.kp-landing-card.practice .kp-landing-cta { color: var(--teal); }
.kp-landing-card.team     .kp-landing-cta { color: var(--fuchsia); }
.kp-landing-card.docs     .kp-landing-cta { color: var(--purple); }
.kp-landing-card.jc       .kp-landing-cta { color: var(--rose); }
.kp-landing-card.insp     .kp-landing-cta { color: var(--sky); }
.kp-landing-cta-arr { transition: transform .2s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1); }
.kp-landing-card--soon { cursor: default; }
.kp-landing-card--soon .kp-landing-cta::after { content: ' \\2014  coming soon'; font-weight: 400; opacity: .7; text-transform: none; letter-spacing: 0; }
.kp-landing-card--soon .kp-landing-cta-arr { display: none; }
.kp-count-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr); gap: 14px; max-width: 640px; }
.kp-count-tile { background: var(--white); border: 1.5px solid var(--bdr-soft); border-radius: 10px; padding: 24px 20px 20px; cursor: pointer; text-align: center; opacity: 0; transform: translateY(16px); animation: cardRise .4s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1) forwards; transition: transform .25s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1), box-shadow .25s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1), border-color .2s; }
.kp-count-tile:nth-child(1) { animation-delay: .06s; }
.kp-count-tile:nth-child(2) { animation-delay: .11s; }
.kp-count-tile:nth-child(3) { animation-delay: .16s; }
.kp-count-tile:nth-child(4) { animation-delay: .21s; }
.kp-count-tile:hover { transform: translateY(-4px); box-shadow: 0 10px 28px rgba(21,14,81,.1); border-color: var(--purple); }
.kp-count-tile--default { border-color: var(--purple); box-shadow: 0 2px 12px rgba(144,57,249,.12); }
.kp-count-num { font-size: 40px; font-weight: 800; color: var(--navy); letter-spacing: -.04em; line-height: 1; margin-bottom: 8px; }
.kp-count-tile--default .kp-count-num { color: var(--purple); }
.kp-count-label { font-size: 11px; font-weight: 700; color: var(--textm); text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: .08em; margin-bottom: 6px; }
.kp-count-time { font-size: 12px; color: var(--textd); font-weight: 500; }
.kp-dr-header { margin-bottom: 28px; }
.kp-dr-eyebrow { font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .18em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--purple); margin-bottom: 8px; }
.kp-dr-title { font-size: clamp(20px, 3vw, 28px); font-weight: 800; color: var(--navy); letter-spacing: -.02em; margin-bottom: 10px; }
.kp-dr-sub { font-size: 13px; color: var(--textm); line-height: 1.7; max-width: 680px; }
.kp-dr-study-header { background: var(--navy); color: rgba(255,255,255,.85); border-radius: 8px 8px 0 0; padding: 12px 20px; font-size: 12px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 4px; }
.kp-dr-study-field span { color: rgba(255,255,255,.5); font-size: 10px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .1em; text-transform: uppercase; }
.kp-dr-table-wrap { border: 1.5px solid var(--bdr-soft); border-top: none; border-radius: 0 0 8px 8px; overflow: hidden; margin-bottom: 20px; }
.kp-dr-table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; font-size: 13px; }
.kp-dr-table thead tr { background: var(--bg-low); }
.kp-dr-table th { padding: 10px 14px; font-size: 10px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .08em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--textd); text-align: left; border-bottom: 1.5px solid var(--bdr-soft); }
.kp-dr-row { border-bottom: 1px solid var(--bdr-soft); cursor: pointer; transition: background .15s; }
.kp-dr-row:last-child { border-bottom: none; }
.kp-dr-row:hover { background: var(--bg-low); }
.kp-dr-row td { padding: 12px 14px; color: var(--textm); vertical-align: top; line-height: 1.4; }
.kp-dr-row td strong { color: var(--navy); }
.kp-dr-row td.cell-highlighted { background: rgba(230,57,70,.12); box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 2px var(--coral); }
.kp-dr-row td.cell-error { background: rgba(230,57,70,.06); box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(230,57,70,.3); }
.kp-dr-context { padding: 10px 16px; font-size: 11px; color: var(--textd); background: var(--bg-low); line-height: 1.65; border-top: 1px solid var(--bdr-soft); }
.kp-dr-flagged-summary { font-size: 12px; color: var(--textd); margin-bottom: 20px; padding: 8px 0; }
.kp-dr-findings { display: none; }
.kp-dr-findings.visible { display: block; }
.kp-dr-findings-head { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; margin-bottom: 16px; border-top: 2px solid var(--navy); padding-top: 20px; }
.kp-dr-findings-label { font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .18em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--textd); }
.kp-dr-carousel-nav { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; }
.kp-dr-nav-btn { width: 32px; height: 32px; border-radius: 6px; border: 1.5px solid var(--bdr-soft); background: var(--white); cursor: pointer; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; color: var(--navy); transition: background .15s, border-color .15s; }
.kp-dr-nav-btn:hover:not(:disabled) { background: var(--navy); color: white; border-color: var(--navy); }
.kp-dr-nav-btn:disabled { opacity: .35; cursor: default; }
.kp-dr-counter { font-size: 12px; font-weight: 700; color: var(--textd); min-width: 48px; text-align: center; }
.kp-dr-finding { border: 1.5px solid var(--bdr-soft); border-radius: 8px; overflow: hidden; animation: ratIn .3s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1); }
.kp-dr-finding-head { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px; padding: 14px 18px; background: var(--bg-low); border-bottom: 1px solid var(--bdr-soft); }
.kp-dr-finding-num { width: 26px; height: 26px; border-radius: 50%; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 800; flex-shrink: 0; }
.kp-dr-finding.error .kp-dr-finding-num { background: var(--coral); color: white; }
.kp-dr-finding.flag .kp-dr-finding-num { background: var(--amber); color: #7a4f00; }
.kp-dr-finding-who { font-size: 13px; font-weight: 700; color: var(--navy); }
.kp-dr-finding-tag { margin-left: auto; font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .1em; text-transform: uppercase; padding: 3px 9px; border-radius: 9999px; }
.kp-dr-finding.error .kp-dr-finding-tag { background: var(--coral-l); color: var(--coral); }
.kp-dr-finding.flag .kp-dr-finding-tag { background: var(--amber-l); color: #7a4f00; }
.kp-dr-finding-body { padding: 16px 18px; }
.kp-dr-finding-problem { font-size: 14px; font-weight: 700; color: var(--navy); margin-bottom: 8px; }
.kp-dr-finding-why { font-size: 13px; color: var(--textm); line-height: 1.7; margin-bottom: 8px; }
.kp-dr-finding-fix { font-size: 13px; color: var(--textm); line-height: 1.7; padding: 10px 14px; background: var(--bg-low); border-top: 1px solid var(--bdr-soft); }
.kp-dr-finding-fix::before { content: 'What should have happened: '; font-weight: 700; color: var(--navy); }
.kp-dr-finding-ref { font-size: 11px; color: var(--textd); margin-top: 8px; }
.kp-dr-ready { font-size: 13px; color: var(--textd); padding: 20px 0; font-style: italic; }
.kp-dr-hub-header { margin-bottom: 28px; }
.kp-dr-hub-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr); gap: 14px; }
.kp-dr-hub-card { background: var(--white); border-radius: 8px; border: 1.5px solid var(--bdr-soft); padding: 20px 20px 16px; box-shadow: 0 2px 8px rgba(21,14,81,.04); }
.kp-dr-hub-card.active { cursor: pointer; transition: transform .25s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1), box-shadow .25s, border-color .2s; }
.kp-dr-hub-card.active:hover { transform: translateY(-3px); box-shadow: 0 10px 28px rgba(21,14,81,.1); border-color: var(--purple); }
.kp-dr-hub-card.soon { opacity: .5; }
.kp-dr-hub-accent { height: 3px; width: 24px; background: var(--purple); border-radius: 2px; margin-bottom: 12px; transition: width .25s; }
.kp-dr-hub-card.active:hover .kp-dr-hub-accent { width: 40px; }
.kp-dr-hub-topic { font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .14em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--purple); margin-bottom: 4px; }
.kp-dr-hub-card.soon .kp-dr-hub-topic { color: var(--textd); }
.kp-dr-hub-title { font-size: 15px; font-weight: 800; color: var(--navy); margin-bottom: 6px; letter-spacing: -.01em; }
.kp-dr-hub-desc { font-size: 12px; color: var(--textm); line-height: 1.65; margin-bottom: 14px; padding-bottom: 12px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--bdr-soft); }
.kp-dr-hub-meta { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; }
.kp-dr-hub-count { font-size: 11px; color: var(--textd); font-weight: 600; }
.kp-dr-hub-cta { font-size: 11px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .05em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--purple); }
.kp-dr-hub-badge { font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .1em; text-transform: uppercase; padding: 3px 8px; border-radius: 9999px; background: var(--bg-high); color: var(--textd); }
.kp-dr-casefile { background: var(--bg-low); border: 1px solid var(--bdr-soft); border-top: 2px solid var(--navy); padding: 18px 20px; margin-bottom: 20px; }
.kp-dr-casefile-label { font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .16em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--navy); margin-bottom: 10px; }
.kp-dr-casefile p { font-size: 13px; color: var(--textm); line-height: 1.7; margin: 0 0 8px; }
.kp-dr-casefile p:last-child { margin-bottom: 0; }
.kp-dr-casefile strong { color: var(--navy); }
@media (max-width: 699px) { .kp-dr-hub-grid { grid-template-columns: 1fr; } }
#kp-phase-team-hub .kp-dr-eyebrow { color: var(--fuchsia); }
#kp-phase-team-hub .kp-dr-hub-topic { color: var(--fuchsia); }
#kp-phase-team-hub .kp-dr-hub-cta { color: var(--fuchsia); }
#kp-phase-jc-hub .kp-dr-eyebrow { color: var(--rose); }
#kp-phase-jc-hub .kp-dr-hub-accent { background: var(--rose); }
#kp-phase-jc-hub .kp-dr-hub-topic { color: var(--rose); }
#kp-phase-jc-hub .kp-dr-hub-cta { color: var(--rose); }
#kp-phase-jc-role .kp-dr-eyebrow { color: var(--rose); }
#kp-phase-jc-role .kp-dr-hub-accent { background: var(--rose); }
#kp-phase-jc-role .kp-dr-hub-topic { color: var(--rose); }
#kp-phase-jc-role .kp-dr-hub-cta { color: var(--rose); }
#kp-phase-insp-hub .kp-dr-eyebrow { color: var(--sky); }
#kp-phase-insp-hub .kp-dr-hub-accent { background: var(--sky); }
#kp-phase-insp-hub .kp-dr-hub-topic { color: var(--sky); }
#kp-phase-insp-hub .kp-dr-hub-cta { color: var(--sky); }
#kp-phase-team-hub .kp-dr-hub-card.active:hover { border-color: var(--fuchsia); }
@media (max-width: 640px) { .kp-landing-grid { grid-template-columns: 1fr; max-width: 100%; } .kp-topic-grid { grid-template-columns: 1fr; } .kp-mode-grid { grid-template-columns: 1fr; max-width: 100%; } .kp-stage { padding: 24px 20px 40px; } }
.te-print-bar { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; padding: 14px 18px; background: var(--bg-low); border: 1px solid var(--bdr-soft); margin-bottom: 40px; max-width: 720px; }
.te-print-bar p { font-size: 13px; color: var(--textm); margin: 0; flex: 1; }
.te-print-btn { border: 1.5px solid var(--navy); padding: 8px 16px; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .05em; text-transform: uppercase; cursor: pointer; white-space: nowrap; background: none; color: var(--navy); display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 7px; transition: background .15s, color .15s; font-family: Arial,sans-serif; }
.te-print-btn:hover { background: var(--navy); color: white; }
.te-print-btn.primary { background: var(--navy); color: white; }
.te-print-btn.primary:hover { background: var(--navy-card); border-color: var(--navy-card); }
.te-section-head { font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .18em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--textd); border-bottom: 1px solid var(--bdr-soft); padding-bottom: 10px; margin: 0 0 20px; max-width: 720px; }
.te-case-intro { max-width: 720px; }
.te-case-intro p { font-size: 15px; color: var(--text); line-height: 1.75; margin-bottom: 10px; }
.te-roster { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(2,1fr); gap: 10px; margin: 20px 0 8px; }
.te-roster-item { background: var(--bg-low); padding: 12px 16px; font-size: 13px; color: var(--textm); line-height: 1.5; }
.te-roster-item strong { color: var(--navy); display: block; margin-bottom: 2px; font-size: 14px; }
.te-block { border: 1px solid var(--bdr-soft); overflow: hidden; margin-top: 20px; max-width: 720px; }
.te-situation { background: var(--white); padding: 22px 24px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--bdr-soft); }
.te-situation-eyebrow { font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .16em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--teal); margin-bottom: 10px; }
.te-situation p { font-size: 14px; color: var(--text); line-height: 1.75; margin: 0 0 10px; }
.te-situation p:last-child { margin-bottom: 0; }
.te-questions { margin: 0; padding: 0; list-style: none !important; }
.te-question { border-top: 1px solid var(--bdr-soft); list-style: none !important; }
.te-question::before { display: none !important; }
.te-q-text { font-size: 14px; color: var(--text); line-height: 1.7; padding: 18px 24px; font-weight: 700; }
.te-kp-toggle { width: 100%; text-align: left; background: none; border: none; border-top: 1px solid var(--bdr-soft); padding: 11px 24px; cursor: pointer; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; gap: 12px; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .08em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--textd); font-family: Arial,sans-serif; transition: color .15s, background .15s; }
.te-kp-toggle:hover { color: var(--navy); background: var(--bg-low); }
.te-kp-toggle svg { flex-shrink: 0; transition: transform .2s; }
.te-kp-toggle.open { color: var(--navy); background: var(--bg-low); }
.te-kp-toggle.open svg { transform: rotate(180deg); }
.te-kp-body { display: none; padding: 18px 24px 22px; background: var(--bg-low); border-top: 1px solid var(--bdr-soft); }
.te-kp-body.open { display: block; }
.te-kp-body p { font-size: 13px; color: var(--textm); line-height: 1.7; margin: 0 0 10px; }
.te-kp-body p:last-child { margin-bottom: 0; }
.te-kp-body strong { color: var(--navy); }
.te-kp-ref { font-size: 11px; color: var(--textd); font-style: italic; margin-top: 10px; display: block; }
.te-reflection { font-size: 15px; color: var(--text); line-height: 1.75; padding: 20px 24px; background: var(--white); border: 1px solid var(--bdr-soft); margin-top: 20px; max-width: 720px; }
.te-meta-row { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 20px; align-items: center; padding-top: 16px; border-top: 1px solid var(--bdr-soft); margin-top: 20px; }
.te-meta-item { font-size: 12px; color: var(--textd); display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; }
.te-meta-item strong { color: var(--textm); font-weight: 700; }
.te-sources { margin-top: 48px; padding-top: 20px; border-top: 1px solid var(--bdr-soft); font-size: 12px; color: var(--textd); line-height: 1.65; max-width: 720px; }
.te-protocol-box { background: var(--bg-low); border-radius: 6px; padding: 14px 18px; margin: 20px 0 8px; font-size: 13px; color: var(--textm); line-height: 1.65; }
.te-protocol-box strong { color: var(--navy); }
.te-protocol-box ul { margin: 10px 0 0 18px; padding: 0; }
.te-protocol-box ul li { margin-bottom: 4px; }
.te-status-label { font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .14em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #7a4f00; margin-bottom: 6px; }
.te-status-box { background: var(--amber-l); border-left: 3px solid var(--amber); border-radius: 0 6px 6px 0; padding: 14px 18px; margin: 20px 0 8px; }
.te-status-box ul { margin: 8px 0 0 18px; padding: 0; }
.te-status-box ul li { font-size: 13px; color: var(--text); line-height: 1.7; margin-bottom: 4px; }
@media print { #site-nav, #site-footer, .kp-hero, .kp-stage > *:not(.phase-active) { display: none !important; } .te-print-bar { display: none !important; } .te-kp-toggle { display: none !important; } .te-kp-body { display: none !important; } body.print-facilitator .te-kp-body { display: block !important; } .te-block { break-inside: avoid; } }
@media (max-width:699px) { .te-roster { grid-template-columns: 1fr; } .te-print-bar { flex-direction: column; align-items: stretch; } }
.jc-scene { max-width: 680px; margin-bottom: 24px; }
.jc-scene[data-state="hidden"] { display: none; }
.jc-scene[data-state="active"] { display: block; animation: qcardIn .38s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1); }
.jc-scene[data-state="review"] { display: block; }
.jc-card { background: var(--white); border-radius: 12px; border: 1.5px solid var(--bdr-soft); overflow: hidden; box-shadow: 0 4px 20px rgba(21,14,81,.07); }
.jc-scene-label { padding: 16px 24px 0; font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .16em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--rose); }
.jc-narration { padding: 10px 24px 0; font-size: 14px; color: var(--textm); line-height: 1.75; font-style: italic; }
.jc-dialogue { padding: 14px 24px 18px; }
.jc-speaker { font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .14em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--textd); margin-bottom: 8px; }
.jc-dialogue p { font-size: 15px; font-weight: 600; color: var(--navy); line-height: 1.55; margin: 0 0 8px; }
.jc-dialogue p:last-child { margin-bottom: 0; }
.jc-choices-wrap { padding: 0 24px 24px; }
.jc-choices-label { font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .16em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--textd); margin: 0 0 12px; }
.jc-choice { display: flex; align-items: flex-start; gap: 12px; width: 100%; text-align: left; background: var(--bg-low); border: 1.5px solid transparent; border-radius: 8px; padding: 13px 16px; margin-bottom: 8px; cursor: pointer; font-family: Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 13.5px; color: var(--textm); line-height: 1.55; transition: background .18s, border-color .18s, transform .2s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1), box-shadow .2s; }
.jc-choice:hover { background: var(--white); border-color: var(--navy); box-shadow: 0 3px 12px rgba(21,14,81,.08); transform: translateX(3px); }
.jc-choice-key { font-size: 10px; font-weight: 700; background: var(--white); border: 1.5px solid var(--bdr-soft); border-radius: 4px; padding: 1px 7px; min-width: 26px; text-align: center; color: var(--textd); flex-shrink: 0; margin-top: 1px; transition: background .18s, color .18s, border-color .18s; }
.jc-choice:hover .jc-choice-key { background: var(--navy); color: white; border-color: var(--navy); }
.jc-feedback-wrap { padding: 0 24px 24px; }
.jc-feedback { padding: 16px 20px; margin: 0 0 16px; font-size: 13px; line-height: 1.7; border-radius: 8px; }
.jc-feedback.good    { background: var(--green-l); }
.jc-feedback.partial { background: var(--amber-l); }
.jc-feedback.fail    { background: var(--coral-l); }
.jc-feedback strong  { color: var(--navy); }
.jc-fb-label { font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .14em; text-transform: uppercase; margin-bottom: 8px; display: block; }
.jc-feedback.good .jc-fb-label    { color: var(--green); }
.jc-feedback.partial .jc-fb-label { color: #7a4f00; }
.jc-feedback.fail .jc-fb-label    { color: var(--coral); }
.jc-feedback p { margin: 0 0 8px; }
.jc-feedback p:last-child { margin-bottom: 0; }
.jc-fb-ref { font-size: 11px; color: var(--textd); font-style: italic; margin-top: 10px; display: block; }
.jc-continue { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; border: 1.5px solid var(--navy); background: none; color: var(--navy); padding: 10px 20px; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .06em; text-transform: uppercase; cursor: pointer; font-family: Arial, sans-serif; border-radius: 6px; transition: background .15s, color .15s; }
.jc-continue:hover { background: var(--navy); color: var(--white); }
.jc-restart { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; border: none; background: var(--navy); color: var(--white); padding: 10px 20px; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .06em; text-transform: uppercase; cursor: pointer; font-family: Arial, sans-serif; border-radius: 6px; transition: background .15s; }
.jc-restart:hover { background: var(--navy-card); }
.jc-tracker { display: flex; gap: 6px; margin: 0 0 32px; }
.jc-dot { width: 8px; height: 8px; border-radius: 50%; background: var(--bdr-soft); transition: background .3s; }
.jc-dot.done { background: var(--navy); }
.jc-dot.current { background: var(--teal); }
.jc-scenario-header { margin-bottom: 32px; max-width: 680px; }
.jc-scenario-eyebrow { font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .2em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--rose); margin-bottom: 10px; }
.jc-scenario-title { font-size: clamp(20px, 2.5vw, 28px); font-weight: 800; color: var(--navy); letter-spacing: -.02em; line-height: 1.1; margin: 0 0 12px; }
.jc-scenario-intro { font-size: 14px; color: var(--textm); line-height: 1.75; margin: 0 0 10px; }
.jc-cast { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 10px; margin: 20px 0 0; max-width: 680px; }
.jc-cast-item { background: var(--bg-low); padding: 12px 16px; font-size: 13px; color: var(--textm); line-height: 1.5; }
.jc-cast-item strong { color: var(--navy); display: block; margin-bottom: 2px; font-size: 14px; }
.jc-cast-you { font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .1em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--rose); display: block; margin-bottom: 4px; }
.jc-cast-role { display: block; }
.jc-ending { max-width: 680px; }
.jc-ending-box { border: 2px solid var(--navy); padding: 24px; margin-bottom: 32px; }
.jc-ending-label { font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .18em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--rose); margin-bottom: 10px; }
.jc-ending-box h3 { font-size: 18px; font-weight: 800; color: var(--navy); margin: 0 0 12px; letter-spacing: -.01em; }
.jc-ending-box p { font-size: 14px; color: var(--textm); line-height: 1.75; margin: 0 0 10px; }
.jc-ending-box p:last-of-type { margin-bottom: 0; }
.jc-review-label { font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .16em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--textd); border-top: 1px solid var(--bdr-soft); padding-top: 24px; margin: 0 0 24px; }
@media (max-width: 699px) { .jc-cast { grid-template-columns: 1fr; } }`;

      </div>
